checks=[]
def check(label,condition,detail=None):
    checks.append({"id":f"J109-{len(checks)+1:03d}","check":label,"passed":bool(condition),**({"detail":detail} if detail is not None else {})})
    if not condition:print("FAIL",label,detail)
def sha(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def getp(obj,p):
    for k in p.split("/")[1:]:
        k=k.replace("~1","/").replace("~0","~")
        obj=obj[int(k)] if isinstance(obj,list) else obj[k]
    return obj
def setp(obj,p,value):
    ks=p.split("/")[1:]
    par=obj
    for k in ks[:-1]:
        k=k.replace("~1","/").replace("~0","~")
        par=par[int(k)] if isinstance(par,list) else par[k]
    k=ks[-1].replace("~1","/").replace("~0","~")
    if isinstance(par,list):par[int(k)]=copy.deepcopy(value)
    else:par[k]=copy.deepcopy(value)
def bind_bytes(b,name):return {"path":name,"bytes":len(b),"sha256":sha(b)}
def check_zip(zz,label):
    names=zz.namelist()
    check(label+" safe unique file members", len(names)==len(set(names)) and all(not PurePosixPath(n).is_absolute() and ".." not in PurePosixPath(n).parts and "\\" not in n and not n.endswith("/") for n in names))
    check(label+" CRC",zz.testzip() is None)
    m=json.loads(zz.read("MANIFEST.json"))
    for v in m["files"]:
        p=v.get("file",v.get("path"))
        data=zz.read(p)
        check(label+" bound "+p,len(data)==v["bytes"] and sha(data)==v["sha256"])
    return m

def encode_like(obj,raw):
    # Choose source serialization by exact match to the starting full object.
    for ensure in (False,True):
        for indent in (2,4,None):
            for trail in ("\n","\r\n",""):
                for eol in ("\n","\r\n"):
                    text=json.dumps(obj,ensure_ascii=ensure,indent=indent)
                    if eol=="\r\n": text=text.replace("\n","\r\n")
                    candidate=(text+trail).encode("utf-8")
                    if candidate==raw:
                        return lambda val, en=ensure, ind=indent, tr=trail, el=eol: (json.dumps(val,ensure_ascii=en,indent=ind).replace("\n",el)+tr).encode()
    return None
p_before={};p_after={};mirror_bindings=[]
for row in sm:
    f=row["replacementPath"]; before=pz.read(row["preservedBefore"]); after=pz.read(row["after"]["path"])
    check("mirror full input binding "+f,len(before)==row["beforeBytes"] and sha(before)==row["beforeSha256"])
    check("mirror full output binding "+f,len(after)==row["after"]["bytes"] and sha(after)==row["after"]["sha256"])
    subops=[x for x in ops108["operations"] if x["file"]==f]
    if f.endswith(".json"):
        ob=json.loads(before);oa=json.loads(after);working=copy.deepcopy(ob)
        encoder=encode_like(ob,before)
        check("mirror baseline serialization "+f,encoder is not None)
        for op in subops:
            check("mirror full before "+f+op["path"], getp(working,op["path"])==op["before"])
            setp(working,op["path"],op["after"])
        check("mirror full forward object "+f,working==oa)
        check("mirror complete forward bytes "+f,encoder(working)==after)
        for op in reversed(subops):
            check("mirror inverse before "+f+op["path"],getp(working,op["path"])==op["after"])
            setp(working,op["path"],op["before"])
        check("mirror complete inverse bytes "+f,encoder(working)==before)
        p_before[f]=ob;p_after[f]=oa
    else:
        text=before.decode()
        for op in subops:
            check("text unique exact before "+f,text.count(op["before"])==1)
            text=text.replace(op["before"],op["after"],1)
        check("text complete forward bytes "+f,text.encode()==after)
        for op in reversed(subops):
            check("text unique exact after "+f,text.count(op["after"])==1)
            text=text.replace(op["after"],op["before"],1)
        check("text complete reverse bytes "+f,text.encode()==before)
        p_before[f]=before.decode();p_after[f]=after.decode()
    mirror_bindings.append({"file":f,"before":bind_bytes(before,row["preservedBefore"]),"after":bind_bytes(after,row["after"]["path"]),"operations":[i for i,x in enumerate(ops108["operations"]) if x["file"]==f]})

def walk(obj,p=""):
    yield p,obj
    if isinstance(obj,dict):
        for k,v in obj.items():
            yield from walk(v,p+"/"+k.replace("~","~0").replace("/","~1"))
    elif isinstance(obj,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(obj):yield from walk(v,p+f"/{i}")
for p,v in walk(aa):
    if (isinstance(v,list) and len(v)==177) or p.count("/")<=1 and "allowance" in p.lower():
        print(p, type(v),len(v) if hasattr(v,"__len__") else "")

endurance 
 Keep advantage on an actual Constitution save to resist Exhaustion; this supplies no save where the governing rule assesses a penalty directly. NEW Endurance/EX58 bridge: keep the same 24-hour provisioning checkpoints, eight-hour Watches, two-active-work ceiling, actual Rest Debt opening, deadline at the end of the second following Watch, and safe eight-hour rest cure. Do not double a Watch, create food/water, lengthen an actual sleep-debt deadline, or treat work as rest.
Maintain two separate feat records, foodWaterPending and sleepPending, each initially 0 and limited to 0 or 1. At a canonical daily checkpoint where food/water shortage is the sole reason for the one expedition-Strain penalty, 0 becomes 1 and that one penalty is omitted; 1 becomes 0 and the original one penalty applies. A day actually meeting both food and water requirements clears foodWaterPending. Another unmet mandatory logistical requirement still causes its original single daily penalty; Endurance does not reduce it or add a second daily penalty. The day remains unsupported wherever its actual requirements are unmet, even when the feat omits a Strain point.
At an actual canonical sleep-debt penalty event, apply the same 0→1/omit, 1→0/apply sequence to sleepPending. Assess each original event once: the unpaid deadline event and a subsequent missed designated Rest Watch follow their own EX58 scheduling; do not create an hourly or per-Watch penalty. Debt remains owed and deadlines reset only as EX58 says. A genuinely completed safe eight-hour rest clears sleepPending and actual Rest Debt, even if it supplies no additional beneficial-long-rest resource recovery. Food/water and sleep credits never offset each other. Other Strain, Exhaustion, air, weather and hazard causes retain their original rules.
Acquisition clears neither existing Strain nor existing debt. If the feat is unavailable at a checkpoint, apply the original cause-specific penalty and clear that cause's pending credit; availability later cannot rewrite the past. Preserve all assessed and omitted events and their causes. This is an explicitly NEW interpretation of 'go twice as long': it halves the frequency of the applicable canonical penalty events, not the elapsed length of every irregular sleep-debt window. Literal older wording and the rejected generic-timer draft remain historical.
forager 
 NEW finite interpretation of the source guarantee: in an accessible non-desert, non-arctic area that actually contains enough recoverable edible food and potable water, spend one active eight-hour forage Watch to gather the normal daily food and water requirement for yourself and up to six other ordinary Small/Medium people. This feat guarantees that result from qualifying available resources without a separate yield roll; it does not guarantee access through a hazard, remove contamination or defeat a resource-specific harvesting gate. Identify a real foraging area and deplete its recorded stocks. The actual expedition procedure supplies food/water units, body-size requirements, carry weight and Supply conversion; this feat creates no extra abstract Supply. Larger or unusual consumers use their actual greater requirement from the same finite quantity, not seven unlimited creature-day portions. Record one feat forage output per holder per24 elapsed hours, shared across all locations, with the window beginning on completion; a second feat owner can work separately only against remaining real stock and their actual time. Deserts and arctic regions use ordinary foraging. A stripped, sealed or foodless place cannot satisfy the prerequisite, and no water appears because food exists. Preserve, transport and consume the actual output normally.
NEW explicit quantity ceiling: the seven ordinary Small/Medium daily food-and-water portions are at most14lb edible food and56pints potable water in one completed480-active-minute Watch. Each pint of water is1lb for the existing cargo accounting. A Large consumer normally uses4lb/16pints from this same stock. These are food/water only, not seven complete Supply Units: no oil, sundries, storage or transport is awarded. If the full guarantee cannot be supported, before work declare an ordinary lawful forage attempt or a smaller supported quantity under this feat; its no-yield-roll benefit may recover only that predeclared real accessible amount. The one-completion/24-elapsed-hour and all actual acquisition/handling restrictions still apply.

 quick_ritualist For a spell you can actually cast through a legal ritual route, halve only the route’s additional ritual time, with resulting additional time at least 5 minutes; retain the base printed casting time in full. NEW measure the result in elapsed seconds and round up to the next whole second so fractions never shorten a required interval. An original 10 minute addition becomes 5 minutes, not half the whole cast. An original shorter addition is not increased: use min(original additional time, max(300, ceil(original/2))). This explicit non-worsening interpretation preserves the minimum for reductions without penalizing a preexisting faster route. Components, formula/repertoire, costs, concentration, helpers and interruptions remain. It grants no Ritual tag or access; the separate proposed Ritual Caster overlay needs its own approval. Apply this additional-time change before any separately legal group active-time adjustment, whose minimum rule remains.
NEW common ritual arithmetic: let B be the spell’s actual base casting labor and A its separately required added ritual labor, in whole seconds. Quick Ritualist changes A to min(A,max(300,ceil(A/2))), leaving B unchanged. This never lengthens an already shorter source procedure. Literal source-required elapsed waiting is not active labor to halve.

This feat-holder contributes as two ordinary assistants within that cap, never as two leads or two physical bodies for a separate location requirement. A lead who owns this feat can supply the time reduction but cannot simultaneously count as two assistants; one person never performs both assigned jobs at once.

from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import zipfile, hashlib, json, io, re, collections, copy, math, fractions
inp = Path("/mnt/data/DD_Feat335_FXC07_08_09_110.zip")
blob = inp.read_bytes()
checks = []
def ck(label, cond, evidence=None):
    row={"id":f"R110-{len(checks)+1:04d}","check":label,"passed":bool(cond)}
    if evidence is not None: row["evidence"]=evidence
    checks.append(row)
    if not cond:
        print("FAIL:",label, evidence)
    return bool(cond)
def H(b): return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def open_data_zip(blob,label):
    z=zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(blob))
    names=z.namelist()
    ck(f"{label} safe unique member paths",len(set(names))==len(names) and all(not PurePosixPath(n).is_absolute() and ".." not in PurePosixPath(n).parts and "\\" not in n for n in names))
    ck(f"{label} CRC", z.testzip() is None)
    return {n:z.read(n) for n in names if not n.endswith("/")}
ck("outer archive exact size/hash",len(blob)==2809655 and H(blob)=="9ffb335abae129304cbc201734f9d20d0e0b98bd4c6619a862bc9c71afcadc26")
outer=open_data_zip(blob,"outer")
for e in json.loads(outer["MANIFEST.json"])["files"]:
    b=outer[e["file"]]
    ck("outer binding "+e["file"],len(b)==e["bytes"] and H(b)==e["sha256"])
npkg=open_data_zip(outer["DD_Feat335_Numeric_Physical_Evidence_109.zip"],"numeric_physical109")
spkg=open_data_zip(outer["DD_Feat335_Spell_State_Evidence_110.zip"],"spell_state110")
print("OUTER RECEIPT",outer["JOINT_FOLLOWTHROUGH_VERDICT_RECEIPT_110.json"].decode())
for label,pkg in [("N",npkg),("S",spkg)]:
    print("\n",label,len(pkg))
    for n,b in pkg.items(): print(n,len(b))
    for n in ("README.md","README.txt","MANIFEST.json","SOURCE_MAP.json"):
        if n in pkg:
            print("\nFILE",label,n,pkg[n].decode()[:30000])

def structure(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict): return {k:(f"dict[{len(v)}] {list(v)[:10]}" if isinstance(v,dict) else f"list[{len(v)}]" if isinstance(v,list) else str(v)[:350]) for k,v in x.items()}
    return type(x).__name__,len(x)

# Search parsed current data strings for a full source section rather than the empty field.
def walk_strings(x,path=""):
    if isinstance(x,dict):
        for k,v in x.items():
            yield from walk_strings(v,path+"/"+str(k).replace("~","~0").replace("/","~1"))
    elif isinstance(x,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(x): yield from walk_strings(v,path+"/"+str(i))
    elif isinstance(x,str): yield path,x
for label,o in [("FXC07",fx7),("FXC08",fx8),("FXC09",fx9)]:
    full=[(p,s[:200],len(s)) for p,s in walk_strings(o) if "FXC-07" in s and len(s)>600]
    print("Embedded possible contract",label,full[:10])

FXC07 areas
0 Native attacks, AC and natural attack results 
  Native attack rules; a margin is not a critical. Expanded19 still needs an actual hit.
1 Core tasks/natural shifts, opposed ties and binary saves 
  Do not transfer task degrees/natural shifts into attacks or binary saves; initiator loses unchanged-state opposed ties.
2 Genuine before-consequence rerolls 
  Named die positions versus whole original save procedure, mandatory replacement and no rollback; current mental predicate is separately retained.
3 Independent native percentage floors and typed distribution 
  Independent floor from the same native eligible subtotal; independent external riders excluded; stable typed allocation and defenses follow.
4 Actual damage, temporary HP, overkill, Wounds and death order 
  Actual lost normal HP plus absorbed temporary HP, not overkill or state assignment; instant death precedes remaining Wound processing.
5 Per-die versus per-packet additions 
  Actual distinct die identities versus flat event/roll additions; rerolls do not create extra dice; no critical doubling of ordinary flats.
6 Current critical ranges and eligible dice 
  Broadest actual range rather than additive threshold steps; native20 and critical-if-hit remain distinct.
7 Healing installments, Wounds, finite reserves and Critical ceilings 
  First eligible installment, spent Hit Dice/rest epochs, blocked/direct recovery, finite source reserves and max(1,maxHP-1) Critical ceiling. Separate vectors do not simulate the combined nested dispatcher.
8 Explicit Strength-fixed branches and real capacity 
  New FN109-01 adds the exact attack-ability/Strength-fixed comparison. Actual carrying and resource capacity use existing body/class sources; growth does not refill.
9 Source-specific no-resource flags, one paid refund and finite spending 
  Preserve flags through descendants; numeric damage alone cannot erase source prohibitions. One actual payment cannot be refunded twice.

# Recover exact contract sections without importing or executing any supplied program.
requirements={}
for code in ("07","08","09"):
    match=re.search(rf"^## FXC-{code} [^\r\n]*\r?\n(.*?)(?=^## |\Z)",contract_text,re.M|re.S)
    assert match
    requirements[code]={"heading":match.group(0).splitlines()[0], "body":match.group(1).strip()}
    print(code,repr(requirements[code]["body"]))
for k,fx in [("07",fx7),("08",fx8),("09",fx9)]:
    ck(f"FXC{k} metadata contract agrees recovered original",fx["contract"]["bytes"]==len(contract_bytes) and fx["contract"]["sha256"]==H(contract_bytes))
    ck(f"FXC{k} exact requirement text is missing in supplied field",fx["completeOriginalRequirement"]=="")
# Fresh current record/rule indexes
joins={}
rulejoins={}
support={}
for key,fx,jk,rk in [("07",fx7,"completeCurrentFeatJoins","completeCurrentSharedRules"),
                    ("08",fx8,"completeCurrentFeatJoins","completeCurrentSharedRules"),
                    ("09",fx9,"currentFeatJoins","currentSharedRules")]:
    joins[key]={j["completeCurrentRecord"]["id"]:j for j in fx[jk]}
    rulejoins[key]={j["completeCurrentRule"]["id"]:j for j in fx[rk]}
    support[key]={j["completeUnchangedReviewedRecord"]["id"]:j for j in fx.get("completeUnchangedReviewedSupportingRecords",[])}
print("\nFEAT IDS")
for key in joins: print(key,list(joins[key]),"SUPPORT",list(support[key]))

Where one predicate combines identity, school, rank or route restrictions, one qualifying route must satisfy that complete predicate; do not combine different routes’ most favorable fields. Distinct alternatives retain their existing AND/OR structure.

Removal, replacement and expiry. Reevaluate the retained grant and repertoire for every new feat acquisition. An actually removed, expired or lawfully retrained-away grant cannot support a new acquisition unless another independently qualifying route exists. Replacing a prepared matrix removes that ordinary prepared route for later snapshots unless another valid route to the spell remains. Temporary suppression or renewable depletion is not removal. Exhausting a genuinely nonrenewable permission follows its actual source: if the permission terminates, it no longer supplies a retained route. Under FS-01, a previously acquired feat keeps its original acquisition snapshot; later prerequisite loss does not erase that historical acquisition or waive any of the feat’s actual ongoing operating conditions.

Unassigned schools and required school choices. Preserve each source spell’s actual school value, explicit null or absent field. An absent, null or unassigned school is not Universal, Enchantment, Evocation, a wildcard, or a school inferred from damage type or a grant’s name. A school-unassigned personal rank-0 grant can satisfy generic spellcasting and cantrip predicates, but cannot itself satisfy a school-specific predicate. Supplementary Spell Focus additionally requires its mandatory permanent selection of an actual school supported by at least one qualifying acquired spell route at that same snapshot. Satisfying its generic spellcasting prerequisite without a supported school choice is insufficient to complete its acquisition. Another genuine route may support that choice. Subsequent loss or suppression of the supporting route does not reselect the school or erase the original acquisition; later benefits still require actual eligible casts through that fixed school.

Authored-candidate evidence versus runtime selection. For authored-candidate qualification, use the exact complete controlling authored spell procedure and its actual source-access binding, supported by the applicable review and fulfillment chain. A preserved earlier blocked catalog snapshot does not override an explicitly reviewed, version-linked operational descendant merely because the original remains unchanged. A separate selectionEnabled:false or disabled runtime adapter does not by itself invalidate that authored evidence. Neither a favorable label nor the existence of a reviewed spell supplies the character’s personal grant or repertoire history. Genuinely unfinished, null, unsupported or operationally unavailable payloads remain unavailable as positive casting witnesses. Preserve original statuses and live selection flags; this acquisition bridge neither enables runtime selection nor promotes another card.

Snapshot and payment. Temporary use blocks may be recorded separately but are not treated as grant removal. Missing grant, preparation, source-version or required-school evidence leaves that predicate unestablished rather than assumed true. Check all other printed ability, level, ownership, credential and earned-opportunity requirements independently. The feat’s own prospective spell grant cannot bootstrap its prerequisite, and one paid opportunity cannot fund two acquisitions.

For the six exploration training entries, snapshot each named skill separately at acquisition: grant proficiency if absent; grant the explicitly printed replacing Expertise only if already proficient. An acquired proficiency does not become Expertise merely because proficiency is later acquired elsewhere. If a fixed grant lacks that branch, duplication yields no replacement skill or Expertise. Skill Expert and Prodigy's ordered explicit selections use their own rules. Study, tutoring, language learning, formula research, copying and crafting are different tasks; a bonus or time discount on one does not migrate to another. Apply the actual minimum phase, calendar, rest and physical requirements.

External-credential evidence — FEAT327-ACQ-04. NEW evidence-contract completion; the four existing feat procedures and their prerequisite alternatives are retained. For frenzied_rage, divine_favor, elemental_body and dragon_blood, evaluate a required external credential under ACQ04-EC1 against the same character, world and ordered acquisition snapshot, using the exact operative prerequisite and source version, linked credential or event evidence, and that world's established authority for recording that fact. A source file establishes wording, not that a particular character acquired a feature, worships a deity, experienced an event or possesses a lineage. A name, nonempty story label, unchecked Boolean, prospective feat benefit or unrelated class/ancestry/item grant supplies no credential. The supporting grant, worship state or exposure event must already qualify before acquisition; same-time events require an authoritative earlier sequence. Worship must be established at that snapshot; exposure is a qualifying prior event, not a requirement to remain exposed. Apply the branch-specific facts and required choices in ACQ04-EC1 without adding doctrine, an exposure threshold, an element-to-plane matching rule or a legacy alias. A conditional, opaque test-world fixture can establish only a CONDITIONAL_ONLY predicate result within that fixture. It supplies no actual campaign history, selectable option, class feature, cosmology approval or entitlement. Missing, unbound, mismatched or insufficient evidence leaves a predicate UNESTABLISHED; affirmative incompatible evidence is CONTRADICTED. Neither result satisfies a required AND node, although a separately satisfied printed OR alternative remains valid. An ESTABLISHED_FOR_SNAPSHOT result requires independently admitted actual-world evidence and is not supplied by this review. Preserve all original source fields, absence/null distinctions, acquisition history and current status/selection flags. All other permanent-ability, level, parent-feat, mandatory-choice and earned-opportunity predicates remain independently required; one paid opportunity cannot fund two acquisitions. Later use still requires the exact feat's ongoing predicates. This evidence policy does not reopen ACQ-03 or change its casting-snapshot rules.

NEW FEAT335-ACQUISITION-AUDIT-01 profile-classification bridge, limited to Weapon Focus and Weapon Mastery: a specific unarmed-strike profile is an eligible choice when it is supplied by an actual current operative source and actual acquired proficiency covers it. The source-listed ordinary Unarmed strike remains a Simple melee training entry even though it is an attack option rather than an inventory object. An actual Simple-weapons proficiency grant covering that entry, or an express unarmed-strike proficiency grant, supplies the required training. An actually acquired Monk unarmed rule changes that same unarmed attack's operative statistics; its die progression alone is not a new paid profile or a free reselection. An acquired Troll Claws trait, explicitly used to make unarmed strikes, is an eligible specific Claws profile when the actor also has that actual unarmed-strike training. Bind both the real trait/body and its covering training grant; Claws alone grants no proficiency. No separate external training credential is necessary when those current-source grants already establish it. Another genuine natural profile remains source-qualified: require its actual profile and covering proficiency, whether an explicit unarmed bridge or another actual grant; neither an umbrella label nor this ruling supplies all-natural-weapon proficiency. No item, anatomy, attack or combined Monk/Claws damage formula is granted. Weapon Focus retains its express ongoing operative-proficiency requirement. Weapon Mastery checks proficiency at acquisition and retains its own matching-profile and total-level-six use predicates, without a new ongoing-proficiency gate. Its nonrepeatability, broadest applicable critical range, natural-19-meets-AC and natural-20-automatic-hit rules are unchanged. 

FS-03 Distinct feat bonuses and retained comparison families 
 NEW joint convention: after eligibility, flat numeric bonuses from DISTINCT feat identities add unless an explicit shared comparison family says otherwise. One feat identity contributes once to the same event/statistic across duplicate representations, sources and owners. This replaces Extended Magic's blanket strongest-same-stat proposal and the conflicting individual statements/examples named in the correction register. It does not rewrite the eight reviewed feats or reviewed maximum-HP components.
Class techniques still perform their own strongest-class-bonus comparison; spells perform their reviewed same-stat comparison; item, attire, tool, facility, cover and item-enhancement contributions retain their own reviewed families. Then add the independently legal feat contribution. A feat-created weapon/armor enhancement explicitly put into an equipment family remains there, not also a feat bonus. Same named condition penalties retain their own comparison rather than becoming additive because a feat created them. Advantage/disadvantage still cancel normally; more sources of advantage do not outvote one disadvantage. Damage resistance of the same scope applies once; immunity retains priority; keep the greatest remaining temporary HP, not their sum. Expanded critical ranges use the broadest applicable range rather than adding chance points. Copies cannot exploit a new family name to duplicate a source. Actual unusual source overrides retain their explicitly narrower predicates. 

FS-04 Native damage, percentages and hostile slows 
 NEW percentage convention: each separately eligible percentage feat computes its own floored increment from the SAME native packet, after actual critical-dice adjustment and its ordinary native ability/flat formula but BEFORE external riders, other percentage increments, saves and defenses. Add the increments; never multiply them together or floor a combined percentage instead of the separate increments. A feat's exact eligible base remains controlling: Anatomy Lore and Precision Strike do not acquire a giant-slayer multiplier on unrelated spells, for example. Added external Smite/sneak/copy/retaliation/rider packets do not enter another feat's native base unless expressly named. Apply a shared-area roll's increment once to that shared roll, then each actual target's save/defenses. For a mixed native packet requiring division, compute the increment once, distribute proportional to nonnegative native typed amounts by largest remainder with original component order breaking ties, and preserve integer totals. Do not add the full increment to each type. Additional dice are added before actual critical doubling and save fractions; a maximized die is not an actually rolled face for rolled-face triggers.
KEEP hostile slow arbitration: evaluate each adverse Speed effect against the same pre-control Speed and take the lowest surviving result; do not add Slasher and Crippling Strike's two -10 penalties, or subtract -10 again after a stronger half-Speed. Distinct records retain their own deadlines and suppression. Root/physical restraint, missing movement modes, terrain costs, burden, systemic Strain and actual Wounds retain their proper independent stages. No movement already spent is refunded. Percent damage addition is not the rule for percentage work/cost/consumption reductions: those use their specifically declared strongest-factor family. 

FS-05 Roll type, reroll window and targets 
 KEEP attacks versus AC, binary saves, fixed-DC Core tasks with natural shifts, and opposed totals with unchanged-state ties lost by the initiator. A +10 attack margin is not a critical. Natural 1/20 on attacks retain their attack rules; saves do not acquire task shifts. A failed-save reroll replaces the whole original one- or two-d20 procedure with the same actual modifiers and advantage/disadvantage, before consequences, unless the exact feat instead replaces a named die position. Use the replacement even if worse; a source cannot reroll its own replacement. Lucky and Elven Accuracy keep their separately specified die-position rules. Costs and prior consequences are not rolled back.
KEEP the current class target convention: 'ally' includes the owner unless the source says OTHER, or real geometry/activity inherently requires a different creature. Do not count the same person twice in 'you and an ally', a team, a flanking pair or an assistance role. 'Enemy' requires an actual current opponent, not every creature in range; meaningful-hostility and anti-farm gates apply only where the source/this register binds resource or encounter rewards. Do not add an enemy or anatomy gate to an unrelated ordinary attack merely as a blanket convention. Source perception, actual reach/line of effect, body eligibility and willing/understood channels remain real.

CURRENT ADOPTED MENTAL-EFFECT PREDICATE (FS-05). A qualifying mental-effect save is the effect's own save to prevent or end Charmed, Frightened, possession, compelled actions/speech, or an explicitly described alteration of thoughts, memories, beliefs, emotions or perceived sensory reality; also include a save directly against psychic damage. Actual concentration maintenance is a separate save, not automatically a mental-effect save because psychic damage caused it. A generic INT/WIS/CHA save, mundane physical hazard or ordinary social check does not qualify solely by ability or theme. Tag the actual effect and outcome before the roll. This is the finite source definition already adopted for Iron Will and Resilient Mind; it supplies no new roll, immunity or concentration permission. 

FS-06 One clock, deadlines, rests and initial uses 
 Use EX58's single elapsed clock, eight-hour Watches, actual task minutes, two-active-work-Watch ceiling and scheduled Rest. A change of scene, gate, holder, location, initiative, class representation or calendar label does not reset a resource. SR/LR in these new procedures mean the actual beneficial completed owner rest, with one beneficial Long Rest per 24 elapsed hours and at most two beneficial Short Rests between beneficial Long Rests. A qualifying long rest restores a short-rest allowance as already governed. Extra safe Rest paying Rest Debt alone provides no second resource recovery. Recipient-rest limits use the actual named recipient's rest epoch.
NEW initial acquisition completion: the newly acquired limited-use feat begins with its specified full finite allowance, unless it expressly starts empty (such as accumulated stacks/charges). Reacquisition/alias duplication cannot initialize it again. An increase in a PB-based maximum increases capacity only, not current unspent uses. Genuine maximum-HP/RP/RC, Hit Die, reserve and slot changes never heal or refill unrelated current values.
Use each explicit own-turn/any-turn/round predicate literally. A per-round allowance has one numbered global-round identity; extra turns do not refresh it. A default 'your turn' feat allowance uses the owner's regular interval unless its exact source direction expressly retains any actual turn. Newly completed missing durations use the next future original regular marker specified by the row, not the already-underway marker, retaining virtual/absolute deadlines across schedule changes. Outside initiative use the governed six-second phase without double ticks. A missed trigger is not queued for later. An early ending does not refund time or a use.

NEW FEAT335-CADENCE-CHECK-01 completion, limited to feat procedures governed by FS-06: an otherwise unqualified "once per turn" allowance is once for that feat owner during each genuine actual creature turn in which its exact trigger legally occurs, whether that turn belongs to the owner or another creature. A separately sourced and actually occurring extra turn has a distinct actual-turn identity and can supply a distinct opportunity for that unqualified allowance; this rule grants no extra turn. Another Action, Bonus Action, attack, Reaction, nested event, target or resumed segment of the same actual turn does not create another turn identity. Returning to or replaying an already recorded turn retains that turn's spent claim. Actual body, movement, attack, casting, payment, causal-root and recipient restrictions still govern independently.

This completion does not replace an expressly regular-turn allowance or FS-06's existing default owner-regular-interval allowance for "your turn". In particular, Savage Attacker retains its stated own/off-turn regular-turn allowances, and Eldritch Combat retains its expressly regular-creature-turn shared embedded-cast cap plus its owner-regular-interval ordinary-append cap. Overrun's per-creature challenge limit belongs to the actual turn containing its qualifying Dash and subsequent movement; another Dash in that same turn does not renew it. Quickened Thought keeps its expressly any-later-actual-turn release window and the shared one-free-stored-release-per-owner/numbered-round cap, not a new bank at each turn. Per-round, encounter, underlying-roll/cast, recipient-rest and source-defined duration/deadline histories are not reset by an extra turn. Outside initiative retain the governed six-second procedure without inventing a turn from each action or counting an initiative transition twice. Source-specific temporal restoration remains separately governed; this completion supplies no rewind or unsupported extra-turn adapter. 

FS-07 Damage, current capacity, healing and causal budgets 
 Keep the reviewed damage/prevention/temp-HP/HP/Wound/death order and exact immunity/resistance/interception windows. Numeric actual damage means actual normal HP lost plus actual temporary HP absorbed, excluding overkill, damage prevented and mere state assignments. That metric never defeats an explicit no-resource origin, no-trigger ward/temp-HP rule or other causal prohibition: only source-qualified events can fund a resource benefit. Preserve those flags through every descendant. Capacity changes are not damage or healing. Ordinary healing observes actual blockers, modifiers, finite reserves and the Critical ceiling; direct state recovery is identified separately and remains subject to broad HP-gain prohibition. A successful stabilization or life-state change does not silently remove Wounds, Prone or another unconsciousness cause.
NEW common anti-loop binding for the new feat rewards: preserve original actor, paid event and target; no staged cooperative threat, vanished/no-resource body, copied packet, reflected retaliation, cost damage or manufactured repeat kill supplies another free resource, attack, healing conversion or reset. Explicit independent printed exceptions are listed in the relevant row, not inferred by name. A root's refunded RP cannot exceed the real unrefunded payment eligible for that particular refund, and headroom still caps restoration. One refund record cannot be spent twice by Arcane Resonance and Arcane Recall. A new feat supplies no RP pool. Reactions require actual availability; specifically passive post-zero registration/recovery exceptions do not grant an Action or Reaction at zero HP. Automatic death processing still precedes later generic regeneration unless the exact source supplies the narrow earlier recovery window.

No second body, observer or extra action is created by self-eligibility. This sentence supersedes any narrower owner-exclusion in the submitted proposal, not the preserved original.

tactical_flanker 1203 
 NEW feat-only geometry: you and one other willing allied, non-Incapacitated creature each threaten the same enemy with an actual legal melee attack, and the straight segment joining your occupied-space centers crosses the interior of that enemy's occupied footprint, placing you on opposite sides. Measure actual occupied bodies on the current map; a large creature needs opposite sides of its whole footprint, not merely two nearby squares. Both participants must perceive the target and be able to attack it; total cover or unusable reach prevents flanking. This geometry itself grants no universal advantage. Once per turn, when either participant hits that enemy with a melee attack, you may assign your feat allowance to add1d6 of one native damage type to that hit before damage is rolled. No Action/Reaction is required, but you must be conscious and able to coordinate. One successful attack may receive only one Tactical Flanker die across all owners; choose its funding owner before rolling. A critical doubles this attack rider die. Misses spend nothing; using your allowance on an ally prevents using it again on your own hit that actual turn. Perception/reach/geometry must hold at the hit.

def ptr(obj, p):
    if p in ("","/"): return obj if p=="" else obj[""]
    for t in p.split("/")[1:]:
        t=t.replace("~1","/").replace("~0","~")
        obj=obj[int(t)] if isinstance(obj,list) else obj[t]
    return obj

Selected numeric/healing current texts
durable 
 Increase Constitution by 1, normally no higher than 20, and recompute the real derived modifier and maximum-HP records without healing. For each actual Hit Die legally spent and rolled to restore HP, NEW: set that die's pre-healing-modifier amount to the greatest of its ordinary die-plus-CON healing amount, twice the current signed CON modifier, and zero. Apply the minimum after the ordinary die-plus-CON calculation, separately per die, before ordinary healing bonuses, reductions, blockers and maximum/Critical caps. The resulting actual HP gain may be less than this raw minimum or zero. It creates no Hit Die, rest permission or automatic restoration of spent dice. Wounds reduce actual Hit Dice recovery under their existing rule, not this individual die's result. Do not floor the die face itself at twice CON or multiply a combined multi-die pool a second time. 

quick_recovery 
 Preserve the current effect: when you legally spend one or more Hit Dice during a completed short-rest recovery procedure, add your current PB once to the total ordinary HP-healing amount for that rest. NEW: commit this single Quick-Recovery-family addition with the first actual Hit Die healing event of that rest; further installments or extra dice in the same rest do not add it again. Zero spent Hit Dice yields no feat addition. Add PB before the ordinary healing reductions, prohibitions and capacity/Critical caps; it is not a promise of PB actual HP restored. The entitlement is spent even if that first event is blocked or entirely excess, and a separate owned converter still needs its own actual rules. Wounds affect recovered Hit Dice under their existing procedure, not a second fee on this bonus. The description/legacy +50-percent out-of-combat regeneration remains preserved but inactive. No free recurring regeneration, extra rest reward or new Hit Dice is created. 

healer 
 NEW full healing procedure: as an Action, spend one real healer's-kit charge to treat one adjacent legally reachable living creature. Choose ordinary stabilization only, or this feat's healing treatment. A full actual kit has ten charges, costs 5 gp and weighs 3 lb under the reviewed item rule; transfer or partial use never refills it. Healing treatment has one entitlement per recipient per that recipient's completed short or long rest, shared across all Healer practitioners. Commit that entitlement and the charge before resolution even if healing is later blocked. If the recipient is dying, first cause an actual dying-to-Stable transition using this paid kit use, then resolve ordinary healing of 2d4 plus the practitioner's signed Wisdom modifier, minimum zero before the normal healing pipeline. One charge funds both parts of this single treatment. If HP gain is blocked, the stabilization remains with its genuine recovery deadline; if positive HP returns, close that obsolete deadline normally. A recipient whose healing entitlement is spent may still receive ordinary kit stabilization for its real charge and Action, without more feat HP. Already Stable creatures can receive the once-per-rest healing but create no new stabilization trigger. This removes no Wounds, Injury, disease or death. Independently owned Field Medic/Field Surgeon triggers require their actual source/event and own limits, with no duplicated kit charge or invented second stabilization. 

field_surgeon 
 When you actually cause a living dying creature to become Stable by a legal Medicine stabilization or a real healer's-kit use, offer that recipient 1 HP. Use the ordinary Action/DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) route or the ordinary Action/one-kit-use route; an independently owned feature may explicitly change that action cost. NEW: treat the 1 HP as a direct state-recovery grant following the completed stabilization, not ordinary rolled healing. Broad cannot-regain-HP restrictions and the living-target gate apply; it receives no ordinary healing bonuses. Record one Field-Surgeon-family entitlement per recipient's own completed-long-rest epoch, shared across all practitioners. Commit it on the eligible offer even when HP gain is blocked. Plant texts
conjure_flora Once per beneficial SR/LR use an Action to create one of the three complete NEW seedling profiles below on visible supported ground within 30 ft and line of effect. It lasts one minute on original source-start markers, needs no concentration, and is a magical nonspell conjured body. Capacity is 1, or 2 at total level 10. NEW second-capacity permission is the single original-use window defined by FLQ-02–03 below; it costs another Action, not another rest use. Each plant gets its own minute. Choose its mode/burst type at creation. One BA may command all active plants within 60 ft able to perceive you, but produces only ONE selected mode outcome per round under the shared offensive-companion allowance. Lash chooses one creature within 10 ft of any participating plant for 1d6 piercing, DEX save half. Spore and entangle use their full profiles. No autonomous turn, opportunity attacks, harvest, loot, Supply, corpse or resource-generating death. Destruction/expiry permanently consumes the body.
FEAT335-FLORA-SOURCE-CHECK-01 / FLQ-04 — NEW split command/phase completion. On the owner's regular turn, first establish a legal shared Bonus Action, an available owner/numbered-round offensive-companion allowance, and actual receipt of the required within-60-ft audible or visible command by each nominated participating plant. At that legal commitment spend the Bonus Action and that one shared offensive allowance once; record the owner, original regular-turn/round identity, participating plant IDs, one chosen mode and target or area, its permitted origin or nominated Lash origins, and the immediately following attached phase. This is a paid pending command, not a Readied action, independent plant turn or extra allowance. It need not be the owner's last voluntary decision that turn. Resolve it once in the attached phase after that same regular turn's actual end processing, using the preserved schedule; an extra turn neither advances nor duplicates that phase. Recheck the surviving unended, unsuppressed, otherwise capable participating plant and the mode's actual target/area, range, perception and line-of-effect requirements. For Lash, an independently legal surviving nominated origin may support the same selected target; losing the selected Spore/Entangle origin does not authorize another origin or mode. For an area, resolve its actual occupants at that phase. If the chosen outcome is no longer legal, close the pending command with no effect, no refund, no new target and no carry to a later phase. After actual receipt, later owner incapacity, Silence, loss of owner-to-plant sight/range/channel, or owner death alone does not retract that command: no continuous concentration/channel/consciousness requirement is added. These states can prevent a NEW command or personal creation/Bloom Burst. An effect expressly cancelling the pending command or disabling/ending its plant still controls. Once committed, no other companion or armed trap can spend the same owner-round allowance, and the phase does not spend it a second time.
KEEP the already-operative SUP-07 floraBody/dc acquisition requirement: at acquisition permanently select and record one actual possessed spellcasting route as this feat's DC provider. Use DC 8 + current PB + that route's current casting-ability modifier; do not invent Wisdom, Spellcraft or another default. This source choice is separate from the later seedling mode/burst-type choice. Ownership initializes only the finite creation allowance and body capacity, with no plant, pending additional creation, command or active effect. All printed acquisition predicates and the actual earned feat opportunity remain required.
FEAT335-FLORA-SOURCE-CHECK-01 / FLQ-01 — KEEP acquisition-only provider selection. A valid paid Conjure Flora acquisition permanently binds its actual qualifying personal casting route and the ability operand supplied by that route. Later loss, expiry, retraining-away or temporary unavailability of that casting grant does not by itself remove Conjure Flora, reselect its provider, prohibit an otherwise legal Flora creation/command/Bloom Burst, terminate an existing seedling or disable an already armed entry trap. Current prepared repertoire, renewable casting charges and RP are not new ongoing Flora prerequisites. This does not qualify a NEW feat acquisition through a grant that is actually gone; ACQ-03 still governs that separate snapshot. Flora is a magical nonspell feat, not a cast through the provider: a spell-only prohibition applies only to its actual scope, Silence still blocks an audible command, and an effect actually suppressing this feat, a plant, its magic or the required action applies under that effect's own rule. NEW explicit lost-provider representation: retain the original provider/ability binding and compute Flora DC as 8 + the owner's current lawful PB + the current modifier of that bound ability; no alternative route, Wisdom default, reconstructed casting entitlement or favorable old ability score is substituted. A missing or invalid original acquisition/provider record is not cured by this rule. Completed results are not recalculated, and existing body HP/save snapshots, deadlines, spent uses and ownership are not reset.
FEAT335-FLORA-SOURCE-CHECK-01 / FLQ-02–03 — NEW second-creation window completion. Each legally completed first creation records one original use ID, its first-plant ID and that plant's original minute deadline, with at most one additional creation unspent. This is a bounded open-use record even when the owner is below total level 10; it is not another rest use or an immediately usable creation at that level. Admit a second creation only while that same first plant remains present and unended, before its original deadline, with the additional creation unspent, current total character level at least 10, actual free plant capacity, and every ordinary Action, ground, range, line-of-effect and use predicate satisfied. The level-10 condition is live at the additional Action, not snapshotted at first creation. Genuine advancement during that open window can make the existing unspent permission usable, but creates no body, charge, healing or new window. Destruction, uprooting, Bloom Burst consumption, expiry or another actual ending of the first plant permanently closes any unused additional-creation permission immediately; its old future deadline does not keep that permission alive. Suppression without an actual ending is not by itself an ending. A legal additional creation consumes that one permission and costs its own Action, but no second rest use; its own minute begins at its own creation. Once the second plant exists, ending the first does not shorten the second's independent deadline. No ended plant, spent permission, missed deadline, level change, recovery, new ID or subsequent body can reopen the old use. A later beneficial rest restores only the ordinary creation allowance under FS-06; a later first creation starts a genuinely new use.
FEAT335-FLORA-SOURCE-CHECK-01 / FLQ-04 — NEW explicit owner/instance continuity. These particular one-minute, nonconcentration Flora bodies do not end merely because their owner is incapacitated or dies; retain the same owner, mode, actual body state, original deadline and no-resource restrictions. No recipient, ally, host, companion or replacement actor inherits control. A received pending command may resolve only at its already attached original phase under the split-command rule, even if the owner's live initiative slot is removed; preserve that one scheduled phase, not a new recurring independent turn. An already armed Trapcasting plant may perform its specified first-eligible-entry exception without a new owner Action/Reaction or remote command channel, even while its owner cannot act or is dead, but it still needs the actual qualifying entry, operative plant/trap, target legality and unspent same-owner numbered-round allowance. Incapacity/death neither refreshes that allowance nor rearms or moves the trap. Preserve the owner's last lawful PB/selected-ability state when its live actor view ends, applying any subsequently due source-defined changes; death is not a zero ability score, a new provider or a freeze of expiring modifiers. Use that continuing lawful record for later Flora DCs; a missing authoritative operand is an invalid/unestablished input, not permission to invent one. Lawful recovery continues the same history without replaying missed phases or refilling anything. Ending a generating plant still ends its own Poisoned/Restrained records immediately. A legally committed Bloom Burst binds its existing DC/type/area event, consumes its body before its one payload, and does not fail merely because that required consumption has occurred; it grants no other action to the ended body. Other independently applicable cancellation, suppression, condition, duration and target rules retain their own actual scope. No Animal Companion release/death rule or standard-companion movement/Dodge/attack is imported into these fixed Flora profiles.
verdant_bond While conscious, exchange brief simple ideas with actual plants within 60 ft you perceive and have line of effect to, using ordinary communication; receive only coarse current fear, hunger, dormancy, not language, intelligence, visions, identity records or omniscient history. A plant may be unresponsive; compel nothing. Gain advantage on actual Nature checks in wilderness terrain, with normal work/senses. While at least one Conjure Flora seedling remains active, gain+1 spell attack/DC under the NEW finite Nature predicate: spells actually cast through your Druid, Ranger or Shaman route, excluding copied profiles; no alias from a plant-themed name. Distinct eligible flat feat bonuses add under FS-03; this same feat contributes only once. In that same active-flora state, difficult terrain caused solely by nonmagical plants costs normal movement; other causes/hazards remain. Once LR spend one uninterrupted minute at a real rooted stand within reach to store a brief message of at most 25 words and one designated known ally identity. For 24 elapsed hours, the first time that ally passes within 10 ft the stand whispers it audibly once and ends the record. Others can hear normally; no remote confirmation or extracted plant memory. Destroying the stand ends the message; no new sentience or cosmology is asserted.
SOURCE ally scope: the owner may be the one chosen message recipient. It triggers on that recipient’s first genuinely future qualifying passage after recording, not immediate creation beside the tree; the audible stored message is not private telepathy or general tree fluency.
trapcasting Conjure Flora OR Verdant Bond qualifies for acquisition, but this feat supplies no missing plants. NEW only actual Conjure Flora profiles below qualify until another source is reviewed. At creation optionally arm a plant’s square as a magical trap using its fixed mode/burst type. Each meaningful hostile’s first actual entry in that square per numbered round, including forced entry but not plant placement, makes DEX save against flora DC; failure applies the profile’s trap result, success none. A legal trigger commits the owner’s shared offensive-companion allowance even on a save; if already spent by a command/other trap, no new trap activation occurs that round. One entrance cannot multiply across plants. Plants are visible, not automatically hidden: Action INT(Investigation) vs flora DC identifies an armed mode; Action DEX with actual thieves’ tools vs same DC disarms one trap without destroying its body, failure gives no automatic explosion. Alternatively, BA consumes one active plant within 60 ft you perceive with line of effect for a 10 ft-radius Bloom Burst at its space: all creatures DEX save,2d6 fixed piercing/poison damage, half success. Same offensive allowance; body ends before burst, cannot also command, and never refunds creation.
NEW phase exception is explicit: its first eligible hostile-entry attack may occur on that actual entry outside the ordinary attached phase, but spends the same owner-round offensive companion allowance immediately. Its paid burst likewise spends that allowance; neither can occur when it is already spent. Placement itself is not an entry-trigger unless this exact procedure expressly says so. General Conjure Flora commands retain their attached phase, shared Bonus Action and sensory channel.
FEAT335-FLORA-SOURCE-CHECK-01 interface binding. Apply Conjure Flora's FLQ-01 provider and FLQ-04 owner/instance continuity completions to these actual Flora bodies. A valid earlier acquisition remains necessary, but continuing possession or immediate usability of its original casting provider is not an additional trap prerequisite. The entry exception is automatic only as already specified; it needs no new command and does not require a living/conscious owner, but uses that same owner's conserved numbered-round offensive allowance. A legally committed ordinary Flora command already spends that allowance before its attached phase, so entry cannot steal or duplicate it. A new personal Bloom Burst still requires its legal Bonus Action, perception, range, line of effect and unspent shared allowance. Ending the first plant by a Burst closes any unused second-creation window; a second plant already created keeps its own original deadline. Verdant Bond-only ownership still creates no Flora body or casting provider, and no new unreviewed conjured-plant profile is admitted.

Rules FS-10, FS-13:
FS-10 Keep one additional FULL Action per creature/numbered round and the existing prohibition on additional Actions generating another additional Action/Bonus Action/Reaction. A source that explicitly grants a separate later Bonus Action has only its named window. Free attacks are not full Actions and cannot recursively grant other free attacks unless an exact existing exception says so. Ordinary Attack Action, card technique, weapon attack, spell attack and cantrip are not interchangeable labels.
Use one physical creature identity, controller record, movement pool and authoritative phase. All controlled companions retain the governed shared Bonus Action command and one offensive/control companion action per owner/round. A source may command two bodies for nonoffensive actions only where stated; it does not grant two offensive actions. The feat owner must actually send the required audible/visible/mental channel; known language, trained signals, Silence and source range matter. Existing Tinker devices use actual reviewed channels and shared budgets.
A bonded animal may be a mount only if its actual body, training, size, seat, tack, load and access meet EX58. It does not gain a second phase for being both companion and mount. Register the applicable EX58 mounted-control phase before operation; do not switch schemas midround to obtain unused budgets. Unmounted feat companions use the explicitly supplied after-owner-regular-turn phase. Mount and rider remain separate targets and actor budgets. Cavalry adds10 to each ACTUAL existing mount locomotion Speed while legally ridden, before applicable burden, terrain and later multipliers; it creates no mode, carrying capacity or engine. Route pace uses the actual resulting limiting profile under EX58, not a second free journey. The rider's being carried is not personal walking, Dragoon Momentum or a movement-cost discount. Mount/dismount uses the actual half-current-walk movement cost, access, one-per-regular-interval allowance and fitting seat; an extra Action does not reset it.
FS-13 ADOPT Appraise's explicit NEW ten-active-minute item identification/appraisal, separate from the current Action-range Identify spell. It can discover actual unprotected operative magical properties and curses as specified in its exact row; it is not a Divination spell by name and does not infer history, secret identities or cosmology. Source-specific identification protections still apply. Use the row's10/15/20/25 standalone DC fallback only where no actual controlling task/item DC exists. Within Haven's appraisal operation use its actual source/task DC (explicit or tier12/13/15/16/18/19), retain the FULL30-minute lot/item procedure, and count the holder's ten-minute inspection as a real nonoverlapping subtask inside it when the same person/work actually satisfies both. No second roll is created by the label.
A ten-minute bargaining feat likewise occupies only its real subtask of the full30-minute Haven disposition; record the remainder for inspection, agreement and transfer. It does not halve those30minutes or remove provenance, hearing, claims, settlement, sponsor/tax/assay or worker shifts. Standalone transactions can use their own actual procedure without inventing compulsory Haven fees. Predeclare the attainable concession, DC, price basis and costs; on success honor the promised outcome, not a newly imposed 'costly agreement' after the roll. No check compels impossible terms, an absent seller or a player belief. Standing remains EX58 -3..+3 with signed per-scene award bounded±2; aliases and background initialization never erase earned history.
Reference value, actual offer, sale gross and net receipts remain separate. Use exact rational currency until the final purchase-ceiling/sale-floor copper rounding. Apply a named discount once to its agreed eligible basis, never to taxes or paid fees by association. Keep the actual greater-of assay rule and sponsor deductions from EX58-EDIT-02; no10% fine cap is revived. Known properties do not mint stock, title, formula knowledge, free service, wages or a guaranteed buyer.

from datetime import datetime, timezone
stamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
outer_name=inp.name
NNAME="DD_Feat335_Numeric_Physical_Evidence_109.zip"
SNAME="DD_Feat335_Spell_State_Evidence_110.zip"
def bound_ref(scope,member,pointer=None):
    pkg=npkg if scope=="N" else spkg
    name=NNAME if scope=="N" else SNAME
    ref={"outerArchive":outer_name,"outerSha256":H(blob),"nestedArchiveMember":name,
         "nestedArchiveSha256":H(outer[name]),"member":member,
         "bytes":len(pkg[member]),"sha256":H(pkg[member])}
    if pointer is not None: ref["pointer"]=pointer
    return ref

    "archive":contract_archive,
    "archiveBytes":Path("/mnt/data/"+contract_archive).stat().st_size,
    "archiveSha256":H(Path("/mnt/data/"+contract_archive).read_bytes()),
    "member":contract_member,"bytes":len(contract_bytes),"sha256":H(contract_bytes),
    "deliveredHereUnchangedAs":"ORIGINAL_GATE_CONTRACT.md",
    "scope":"Recovered exact original requirement authority from the earlier supplied FT84 input. Only FXC-07/08/09 are assessed here; old counts and unrelated gate/delivery language remain historical."
}
repair_ops=[]
for code,fx,scope,pkg in [("07",fx7,"N",npkg),("08",fx8,"N",npkg),("09",fx9,"S",spkg)]:
    member=f"FXC{code}.json"
    raw=pkg[member]
    before=b'"completeOriginalRequirement": ""'
    ck(f"FXC{code} unique raw empty requirement field",raw.count(before)==1)
    after_span=b'"completeOriginalRequirement": '+json.dumps(requirements[code]["body"],ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    pos=raw.index(before)
    virtual=raw[:pos]+after_span+raw[pos+len(before):]
    desired=copy.deepcopy(fx);desired["completeOriginalRequirement"]=requirements[code]["body"]
    ck(f"FXC{code} documentary patch changes only that full value",json.loads(virtual)==desired)
    restored=virtual[:pos]+before+virtual[pos+len(after_span):]
    ck(f"FXC{code} documentary reverse restores complete original bytes",restored==raw)
    repair_ops.append({
        "id":f"FT110-DOC-{code}","scope":"PROPOSED_DOCUMENTARY_SOURCE_JOIN_ONLY_NOT_APPLIED",
        "target":bound_ref(scope,member,"/completeOriginalRequirement"),
        "op":"replace","before":"","after":requirements[code]["body"],
        "reason":"The exact named requirement field is empty in the submitted map. The exact original contract was recovered from a previously supplied archive matching this map's own size/hash. This fills evidence text only; it does not add or revise a gameplay rule.",
        "source":{**contract_binding,"heading":requirements[code]["heading"]},
        "exactRawUtf8Replacement":{
            "startByte":pos,"beforeBytes":len(before),"before":before.decode(),
            "afterBytes":len(after_span),"after":after_span.decode()
        },
        "virtualSuccessor":{"bytes":len(virtual),"sha256":H(virtual),"deliveredCompleteFile":False,
                           "meaning":"Entire virtual JSON bytes computed by this one exact raw-field replacement; only complete values and references are delivered."},
        "inverse":{"op":"replace","path":"/completeOriginalRequirement",
                   "before":requirements[code]["body"],"after":""}
    })

review_text=f"""# Feat335 Follow-through110 — FXC07 / FXC08 / FXC09

## Verdict

**The three named authored-evidence requirements are covered at their disclosed premises.** No additional gameplay-rule repair or invalid current scoped boundary example was identified. A documentary omission must be corrected: all three submitted maps contain an empty `/completeOriginalRequirement`.

This is an assessment of these named evidence requirements, not full335 semantic acceptance, canonical application or a claim that the helper code implements the game.

## Exact requirement recovery

FXC07.json, FXC08.json and FXC09.json each have `completeOriginalRequirement: ""`. The maps bind an original 7,680-byte acceptance contract but neither new nested ZIP includes its full file as a separate member.

The exact original was found in the earlier supplied `{contract_archive}` at `{contract_member}`. It has SHA256 `{H(contract_bytes)}`, matching all three current declared contract bindings. The file is copied unchanged as `ORIGINAL_GATE_CONTRACT.md`.

`DOCUMENTARY_REPAIRS.json` contains three full-before/full-after replacements from the exact submitted map bytes. The full original paragraphs, raw UTF-8 spans, complete virtual successor hashes and inverse values are supplied. They change evidence text only. Both the pointer and raw-span forms represent the same changes; do not apply both.

No empty field is silently described as complete. The recovered exact authority makes the substantive scope comparison possible without reconstructing requirements from summary prose or changing their meaning.

## FXC07 — numeric/native/healing evidence

Ten areas cover the exact requirement: native attacks/AC; Core task shifts; opposed ties and binary saves; before-consequence rerolls; independent percentage floors and typed allocation; actual damage, temporary HP and overkill; per-die versus per-packet additions; critical ranges; Wounds/Critical healing limits, reserves and rest history; Strength-fixed/capacity boundaries; source-specific no-resource ancestry.

The 36 complete resolution85 and 56 healing89 cases remain exact, alongside the current full records and inherited original/additional semantic reviews. The preserved spear example uses native19 and increments9/9/3, then resistance, temporary HP and normal HP; it distinguishes actualDamage15 from overkill5. def collected_refs(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict):
        if "nestedArchiveMember" in x and "member" in x and "sha256" in x:
            yield x
        for v in x.values(): yield from collected_refs(v)
    elif isinstance(x,list):
        for v in x: yield from collected_refs(v)
